Do not filter, skip, or reinterpret.\n#\n# Exercises ./ledger against a fresh database and compares every command's exit code and standard\n# output with the known answer below. Standard error is never compared.\n#\n#   sh sources/run.sh                    from the application root (how `drydock uat` runs it)\n#   sh uat/LayersTesting/bin/run.sh APP  from anywhere, grading the application root APP\n#\n# Database: DATABASE_URL when the caller sets it (for example a PostgreSQL DSN), otherwise a\n# SQLite file in a fresh temporary directory. Every run starts with `ledger init`, so the grader\n# never depends on prior state. Exit 0 only when every check passes.\nset -u\n\nHERE=$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\nAPP=$(cd \"${1:-.}\" && pwd)\nif [ -f \"$HERE/seed_accounts.csv\" ]; then SEED=$HERE; else SEED=$(cd \"$HERE/../sources\" && pwd); fi\nLEDGER=\"$APP/ledger\"\nWORK=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$WORK\"' EXIT INT TERM\nDATABASE_URL=${DATABASE_URL:-$WORK/ledger.db}\nexport DATABASE_URL\n\nif [ ! -x \"$LEDGER\" ]; then\n    echo \"error: no executable ./ledger at the application root $APP.\" >&2\n    echo \"The deliverable is an executable named ledger at the application root.\" >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\nPASSED=0\nFAILED=0\n\n# check <id> <expected exit> <ledger args...>  — expected standard output on stdin.\ncheck() {\n    id=$1\n    want_rc=$2\n    shift 2\n    cat > \"$WORK/want\"\n    (cd \"$APP\" && \"$LEDGER\" \"$@\") > \"$WORK/got\" 2> \"$WORK/err\"\n    rc=$?\n    if [ \"$rc\" -eq \"$want_rc\" ] && cmp -s \"$WORK/want\" \"$WORK/got\"; then\n        PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))\n        echo \"PASS $id: ledger $*\"\n    else\n        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))\n        echo \"FAIL $id: ledger $*\"\n        echo \"  exit: expected $want_rc, got $rc\"\n        diff \"$WORK/want\" \"$WORK/got\" | sed 's/^/  /'\n        sed 's/^/  stderr: /' \"$WORK/err\"\n    fi\n}\n\n# ── A. Schema lifecycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ncheck A1 0 init < /dev/null\ncheck A2 0 count <<'EOF'\ntable,rows\naccounts,0\ncategories,0\ntransactions,0\nEOF\n\n# ── B. Bulk load of the known dataset ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ncheck B1 0 load accounts \"$SEED/seed_accounts.csv\" <<'EOF'\ntable,loaded\naccounts,4\nEOF\ncheck B2 0 load categories \"$SEED/seed_categories.csv\" <<'EOF'\ntable,loaded\ncategories,7\nEOF\ncheck B3 0 load transactions \"$SEED/seed_transactions.csv\" <<'EOF'\ntable,loaded\ntransactions,120\nEOF\ncheck B4 0 count <<'EOF'\ntable,rows\naccounts,4\ncategories,7\ntransactions,120\nEOF\n\n# ── C. Reports over the seeded rows ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ncheck C1 0 report balances <<'EOF'\naccount,name,transactions,balance\nDRAWER1,Front Drawer,39,2299.38\nDRAWER2,Back Drawer,39,1378.32\nPETTY,Petty Cash,21,306.25\nSAFE,Store Safe,21,250.00\nTOTAL,,120,4233.95\nEOF\ncheck C2 0 report categories <<'EOF'\ncategory,description,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\nOPENING,Opening balance,2,5200.00,0.00,5200.00\nPAYROLL,Payroll,6,0.00,7200.00,-7200.00\nREFUND,Customer refunds,12,0.00,101.25,-101.25\nRENT,Rent,3,0.00,2700.00,-2700.00\nSALES,Sales receipts,64,9578.95,0.00,9578.95\nSUPPLY,Supplies,15,0.00,543.75,-543.75\nTRANSFER,Internal transfer,18,5100.00,5100.00,0.00\nTOTAL,,120,19878.95,15645.00,4233.95\nEOF\ncheck C3 0 report monthly <<'EOF'\nmonth,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\n2026-01,40,9659.45,5215.00,4444.45\n2026-02,40,4979.65,5215.00,-235.35\n2026-03,40,5239.85,5215.00,24.85\nTOTAL,120,19878.95,15645.00,4233.95\nEOF\n\n# ── D. Commands read CSV on\nstandard output, diagnostics on standard error, and exit codes 0 to 4. Every command runs as one\ndatabase transaction. All persistence goes through the shipped persistence library, and the\ndatabase is selected by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n| Technology | Rigging | Role |\n|------------|---------|------|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | `python.md` | Implementation language. |\n| SQLite | `sqlite.md` | Database engine, reached through `persistence.open_database` and the shipped SQLite engine. |\n| PostgreSQL | `python.md` (persistence contract) | Selected only by a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN through the same shipped library. Not verified by any acceptance check. |\n| CLI | `cli.md` | The `ledger` executable at the application root. |\n| pytest | `python.md` | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `common.md` | `bin/test.sh`, `bin/sync.sh`, the `ledger` launcher, and the supplied grader. |\n\nDependency management uses `uv`: `pyproject.toml` declares the project and its dependencies,\n`uv.lock` is the committed lock file, and `.venv/` is the environment directory created by\n`uv venv` (gitignored).\n\n## Module Layout\n\n- `ledger`: POSIX launcher at the application root. It changes to the application root and runs `cli.main` under `.venv`.\n- `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`: dependency manifest and committed lock file. Runtime dependency `psycopg[binary]`; dev group `pytest`.\n- `.venv/`: environment created by `uv venv`. Not committed.\n- `bin/`: POSIX launchers. `bin/sync.sh` creates `.venv` and installs from `uv.lock`; `bin/test.sh` activates `.venv` and runs `pytest -q`.\n- `config.py`: database target resolution from the environment.\n- `cli/`: `main(argv)` entry point, `cli_runner` test client, command dispatch, and CSV output.\n- `commands/`: one module per command group (accounts, categories, transactions, transfers, loads, reports). Each command body runs inside the command transaction wrapper.\n- `persistence/` and `schema.sql`: generated from `DATABASE.md` at the DATABASE step.\n- `tests/`: pytest suite for the application. `tests/test_foundation.py` covers the project manifest, lock file, environment, and launchers.\n\n## Boundaries\n\n- Call direction is `cli` to `commands` to `persistence`. The `cli` package never opens a database.\n- `commands` reach storage only through `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`, obtained from `persistence.open_database`.\n- Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any PostgreSQL driver. Only the shipped engines under `persistence/` do.\n- `config.py` reads the environment and returns the selected target as a string. It never opens a connection.\n- No command reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file except through `ledger load`.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|-------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated and shipped) | Driver APIs inside `persistence/sqlite.py` and `persistence/postgres.py` only |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | `os.environ` for `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | None. Seed files are read only by `ledger load`, through `commands/` |\n| External service | None | None |\n\n## Transaction and Balance Boundary\n\n- The `commands` layer opens one transaction per command and commits it only after every change succeeds.\n- Before commit, the balance rule is checked for every affected account. A negative balance rolls the command back and exits 3.\n- Concurrency relies on the database's own transaction isolation. No application-level locking is added.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Application code never opens a database driver outside the shipped persistence library.\n- Money is stored and computed as integer cents at every layer.\n- No command writes outside the application root and its temporary test database.\n- No report row, count, or total is embedded in code.\n----\ncommit 12d62d4a9306f0e141449923232506d07edf6b88\nAuthor: Ed Barlow <edward.m.barlow@gmail.com>\nDate:   Tue Oct 20 11:15:16 2026 -0400\n\n    Build block-2: DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md [built]\n    \n    Build-Lineage-Id: 27674c7f29e84b528c96877196550e5b\n    Build-Run-Id: 24dd8ae2d90549419403ebf74d66ffd4\n\n cli/__init__.py         |  15 ++\n cli/__main__.py         |   7 +\n cli/commands.py         |  11 ++\n cli/parser.py           |  11 ++\n cli/testing.py          |  16 +++\n ledger                  |   3 +\n persistence/__init__.py |   6 +\n persistence/db.py       | 375 ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++\n persistence/postgres.py |  74 ++++++++++\n persistence/schema.py   | 155 ++++++++++++++++++++\n persistence/sqlite.py   |  67 +++++++++\n persistence/testing.py  |  26 ++++\n pyproject.toml          |   1 +\n schema.sql              |   6 +\n tests/test_cli.py       |  59 ++++++++\n 15 files changed, 832 insertions(+)\napp.db\n3.12.13 (main, Mar  3 2026, 14:59:34) [Clang 21.1.4 ]","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e5a5083c-44d2-41c8-8cba-875930eb5958","uuid":"e404ca17-9f8b-4a05-ab8c-f92bf089eee1","timestamp":"2026-10-20T15:15:54.583Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Generated by Drydock from DATABASE.md. Edit the declaration, not this file.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"accounts\" (\"account\" VARCHAR(12) PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, \"name\" VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL);\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"categories\" (\"category\" VARCHAR(12) PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, \"description\" VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL);\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"transactions\" (\"txn_id\" INTEGER PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, \"date\" VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, \"account\" VARCHAR(12) NOT NULL REFERENCES \"accounts\"(\"account\"), \"category\" VARCHAR(12) NOT NULL REFERENCES \"categories\"(\"category\"), \"amount\" INTEGER NOT NULL, \"memo\" VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL DEFAULT '');\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"transactions_account_idx\" ON \"transactions\" (\"account\");\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"transactions_category_idx\" ON \"transactions\" (\"category\");\n----\n# ARCHITECTURE: Cash Ledger\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Module layout, boundaries, and technology decisions for the command-line cash ledger. |\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `ledger` executable is a command-line cash ledger for a small shop. Commands read CSV on\nstandard output, diagnostics on standard error, and exit codes 0 to 4. Every command runs as one\ndatabase transaction. All persistence goes through the shipped persistence library, and the\ndatabase is selected by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n| Technology | Rigging | Role |\n|------------|---------|------|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | `python.md` | Implementation language. |\n| SQLite | `sqlite.md` | Database engine, reached through `persistence.open_database` and the shipped SQLite engine. |\n| PostgreSQL | `python.md` (persistence contract) | Selected only by a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN through the same shipped library. Not verified by any acceptance check. |\n| CLI | `cli.md` | The `ledger` executable at the application root. |\n| pytest | `python.md` | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `common.md` | `bin/test.sh`, `bin/sync.sh`, the `ledger` launcher, and the supplied grader. |\n\nDependency management uses `uv`: `pyproject.toml` declares the project and its dependencies,\n`uv.lock` is the committed lock file, and `.venv/` is the environment directory created by\n`uv venv` (gitignored).\n\n## Module Layout\n\n- `ledger`: POSIX launcher at the application root. It changes to the application root and runs `cli.main` under `.venv`.\n- `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`: dependency manifest and committed lock file. Runtime dependency `psycopg[binary]`; dev group `pytest`.\n- `.venv/`: environment created by `uv venv`. Not committed.\n- `bin/`: POSIX launchers. `bin/sync.sh` creates `.venv` and installs from `uv.lock`; `bin/test.sh` activates `.venv` and runs `pytest -q`.\n- `config.py`: database target resolution from the environment.\n- `cli/`: `main(argv)` entry point, `cli_runner` test client, command dispatch, and CSV output.\n- `commands/`: one module per command group (accounts, categories, transactions, transfers, loads, reports). Each command body runs inside the command transaction wrapper.\n- `persistence/` and `schema.sql`: generated from `DATABASE.md` at the DATABASE step.\n- `tests/`: pytest suite for the application. `tests/test_foundation.py` covers the project manifest, lock file, environment, and launchers.\n\n## Boundaries\n\n- Call direction is `cli` to `commands` to `persistence`. The `cli` package never opens a database.\n- `commands` reach storage only through `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`, obtained from `persistence.open_database`.\n- Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any PostgreSQL driver. Only the shipped engines under `persistence/` do.\n- `config.py` reads the environment and returns the selected target as a string. It never opens a connection.\n- No command reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file except through `ledger load`.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|-------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated and shipped) | Driver APIs inside `persistence/sqlite.py` and `persistence/postgres.py` only |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | `os.environ` for `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | None. Seed files are read only by `ledger load`, through `commands/` |\n| External service | None | None |\n\n## Transaction and Balance Boundary\n\n- The `commands` layer opens one transaction per command and commits it only after every change succeeds.\n- Before commit, the balance rule is checked for every affected account. A negative balance rolls the command back and exits 3.\n- Concurrency relies on the database's own transaction isolation. No application-level locking is added.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Application code never opens a database driver outside the shipped persistence library.\n- Money is stored and computed as integer cents at every layer.\n- No command writes outside the application root and its temporary test database.\n- No report row, count, or total is embedded in code.\n----\ncommit 12d62d4a9306f0e141449923232506d07edf6b88\nAuthor: Ed Barlow <edward.m.barlow@gmail.com>\nDate:   Tue Oct 20 11:15:16 2026 -0400\n\n    Build block-2: DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md [built]\n    \n    Build-Lineage-Id: 27674c7f29e84b528c96877196550e5b\n    Build-Run-Id: 24dd8ae2d90549419403ebf74d66ffd4\n\n cli/__init__.py         |  15 ++\n cli/__main__.py         |   7 +\n cli/commands.py         |  11 ++\n cli/parser.py           |  11 ++\n cli/testing.py          |  16 +++\n ledger                  |   3 +\n persistence/__init__.py |   6 +\n persistence/db.py       | 375 ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++\n persistence/postgres.py |  74 ++++++++++\n persistence/schema.py   | 155 ++++++++++++++++++++\n persistence/sqlite.py   |  67 +++++++++\n persistence/testing.py  |  26 ++++\n pyproject.toml          |   1 +\n schema.sql              |   6 +\n tests/test_cli.py       |  59 ++++++++\n 15 files changed, 832 insertions(+)\napp.db\n3.12.13 (main, Mar  3 2026, 14:59:34) [Clang 21.1.4 ]","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01JRatJFUPhUD5nhtTuKKPAL","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}
